# ArcheAge 1.2 UI native API (what the original Lua UI expects)

Generated by `cargo run -p aa-lua --bin aa-lua -- record` (crate `aa-lua`). Machine-readable: `data/ui-api.json`. Sources: a static scan of the bytecode of all 731 UI scripts, a run of every screen's scripts in the client's load order with a recording proxy behind every native name (plus one call of every handler they register), and the native function and event tables extracted from `x2game.dll` (`crates/aa-lua/data/x2game.json`).

Columns: **sites** = call/read sites in the bytecode (all code paths), **argc** = argument counts seen (most common first, `self` excluded), **params** = parameter names the client registers natively, **types** = argument types seen at run time (`widget` = a widget or drawable, `native` = another native value), **L** = needed by the login stage (world/character select/create). Names are ranked by sites.

## Contents

0. Login stage at a glance
1. Load order
2. Widget toolkit (widget types, drawables, handlers, methods)
3. X2 game API
4. CryEngine API
5. Other native functions and values
6. UI events
7. Native bind groups (x2game.dll)
8. Run errors
9. Load-order check against the real client

## 0. Login stage at a glance

What world select, character select and character create (and the login screen) need natively. Details in the sections below.

**Widget types:** `label`, `button`, `emptywidget`, `window`, `textbox`, `checkbutton`, `slider`, `gametooltip`, `editbox`, `listctrl`, `tab`, `listbox`, `message`, `folder`, `webbrowser`, `unitframetooltip`, `paintcolorpicker`, `circlediagram`

## 1. Load order

Every screen first runs `scriptsbin/x2ui/baselib/locale_helper.alb` once (UI state init), then these units in order (a `toc.g` = its listed files; see `docs/aa-lua.md`). The four login screens each start from `LoadBaseLib` again.

* **character_create**: logic, baselib, components, option, loginstage/character_create
* **character_select**: logic, baselib, components, option, loginstage/character_select, secondpassword
* **loading**: scriptsbin/globalui/loading
* **login**: logic, baselib, components, option, loginstage/login
* **world**: logic, baselib, components, option, ui_management.alb, abilitychange, achievement, auction, battlefield, centermessage, changeitemlook, chat, chracterinfo, clientdrivenindun, combattext, commonfarm, crafting, customizing, dialoghandler, expedition, housing, hud, indunchannelselect, ingameshop, inventory, loot, mailbox, map, messageboximpl, music, nation, portal, premiumservice, questcontext, racing, raidframe, raidteammanager, ranking, relationship, repair, secondpassword, sensitiveoperation, skill, slave, specialty, store, territory, trade, tutorial, unitframe, usertrial, webbrowser, npc_common.alb, character_ability.alb, faction.alb, interaction.alb, npc_quest_interaction.alb, store_backpack.alb, transfer.alb, dynamic_action_bar.alb, ucc.alb, demo.alb, npc_interaction.alb, renew_item.alb
* **world_select**: logic, baselib, components, option, loginstage/world_select

Recorder runs:

| stage | scripts | scripts with errors | handler calls | handler errors |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| login | 122 | 0 | 474 | 15 |
| world_select | 122 | 0 | 400 | 45 |
| character_select | 127 | 0 | 539 | 17 |
| character_create | 140 | 0 | 1000 | 69 |
| world | 606 | 10 | 7328 | 572 |
| loading | 4 | 0 | 5 | 1 |

Member tables list every native function of the table in the real client (reference capture) plus whatever the scripts use: `~name` = native but never used by the scripts, `!name` = used by the scripts but not a native function of the table (nil in the client: a script bug, a guarded `if X.f then`, or a script-added field). **params** come from the matching registration in `x2game.dll` (section 7).

### Values (constants, enums, tables)

Native globals the scripts read but never assign or call (`ALIGN_CENTER`, `UIC_PLAYER_UNITFRAME`, `RACE_NUIAN`, ...) with the value in the real client where captured (`= value`; tables in `data/ui-api.json`). Format: `name` sites [L] [= value].

### Read but absent in the real client

Globals the scripts read, no script assigns, and the real client's UI state does not have at world select: script bugs or guarded reads (`if foo ~= nil`), or values only created in game. Leave them nil unless the in-game capture shows otherwise.

## 6. UI events

The client's event table has 626 events (id = index, used by `FireUIEvent`; e.g. 70 `ENTERED_LOGIN`, 477 `ENTERED_WORLD_SELECT`, 78 `ENTERED_WORLD`). Scripts subscribe with `widget:RegisterEvent(name)` (usually via the script helper `RegistUIEvent(widget, {NAME = fn})`) and receive them in the widget's `OnEvent` handler as `(this, event, ...)`. ## 7. Native bind groups (x2game.dll)

Each script-bind class registers its functions in one function of `x2game.dll`. The owner is the real-client class (reference capture) whose native method list contains most of the group: an X2 table, `UIParent`, a widget type (its own methods), `widget` (the ~90 methods every widget type has), a drawable (`ImageDrawable` ...) or `style` (text style objects). Groups with owner `?` belong to the CryEngine/System script state, not the UI. Full lists with parameters are in `data/ui-api.json` (`native_bind_groups`).

## 8. Run errors

Where the scripts stopped in the recorder run (all stages), most common first. They mark the proxy's limits (a native value that must be a real number, string or table) or a load-order dependency; the full list is in `data/ui-api.json` (`load_errors`).

* 2x `attempt to compare number with string`
* 2x `attempt to get length of field 'window' (a nil value)`
* 1x `attempt to compare table with number`
* 1x `attempt to get length of local 'tabButtonIdxs' (a nil value)`
* 1x `attempt to index field 'level' (a nil value)`
* 1x `attempt to index global 'watchTargetFrame' (a nil value)`
* 1x `attempt to perform arithmetic on local 'maxHp' (a string value)`
* 1x `bad argument #5 to 'sformat' (number expected, got nil)`
